Title: The Psalms of David imitated in the language of the New Testament, and apply'd to the Christian state and worship; by I. Watts
Creator: Isaac Watts (1674-1748)
Publisher: London: printed for J. Clark, at the Bible and Crown in the Poultry; R. Ford, at the Angel in the Poultry; and R. Cruttenden, at the Bible and Three Crowns in Cheapside
Publication Date: 1719 (the host's date; the last numeral of the title-page year is garbled in the scan)
Rights: Public Domain (published 1719, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item chrtwimi00watt (https://archive.org/details/chrtwimi00watt), the item's own chrtwimi00watt_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1719. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 5d41406e8fe649e49ef55d8897fb88138c833658d57b37bd57eff4dcdcabf094.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary scan; readable with the long s as f. The title-page year reads '171?' in the OCR.
Content note: Watts's Christian rendering of the Psalms for congregational singing, with his preface, 'an Enquiry into the right Way of fitting the Book of Psalms for Christian Worship'; the companion to the Hymns that changed Dissenting worship.
